- **Plan Critic**: Phase 6.0, second step — stress-tests and pre-mortems the solution plan on a different model vendor than the planner, so the plan is attacked by a model that does not share its blind spots. - **plan-premortem**: Pre-execution plan critic. Give it a plan (the steps, files to touch, and assumptions) plus repo access, and it writes the premortem — "this plan was executed and it failed; here is the incident report." It verifies the plan's factual claims against the actual repo, surfaces load-bearing unverified assumptions, names the step most likely to blow up, and says what to de-risk first. Use before starting any multi-file or multi-step task; it is the cheapest insurance against a plan that under-counted the terrain. - **plan-reader**: Read-only summarizer for a Terraform or OpenTofu plan.json, a rendered Kubernetes diff, or Ansible check-mode output. ([iuliandita/idstack](https://github.com/iuliandita/idstack/blob/HEAD/harness/opencode/agents/plan-reader.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **plan-reader**: Reads docs/plans/&lt;FEATURE-ID&gt;-plan.md and returns a compact digest — Goal, Task list, Decision State, Validation status, and Traceability gaps — without loading the whole plan into the caller's context. Use before review/execute, or whenever a stage needs the plan's shape but not its full body. ([lucasAguiar11/agent-skills](https://github.com/lucasAguiar11/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/workflow-kit/agents/plan-reader.md))
- **plan-readiness-reviewer**: Independent reviewer that decides whether a change plan is ready to implement, using the plan-readiness-review method, and writes the change's readiness.md with "- Status: APPROVED | NEEDS-REVISION | BLOCKED". It is not the plan's author and ignores any verdict the caller suggests. Use when a change's tasks are written and the plan needs its readiness gate, or when the user asks "is this plan ready?" for a change folder. ([chacal88/devkit](https://github.com/chacal88/devkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/core/agents/plan-readiness-reviewer.md))
- **plan-reality-auditor**: Checks that a change plan's claims about the current code — the "current behavior", "context" or "what exists" statements in proposal.md/design.md — are still true today, citing file:line, and returns an ACCURATE/OUTDATED verdict. Read-only. Use before a plan review or before implementing an older plan — "is this plan still accurate?", "audit the current state", "has the code drifted since we planned?". ([chacal88/devkit](https://github.com/chacal88/devkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/core/agents/plan-reality-auditor.md))
- **plan-reality-checker**: Verifies that a planning doc's claims about the codebase are actually true. For every "we'll extend X" / "this fits Y pattern" / "use existing Z", searches the repo to confirm or refute. Catches planner hallucinations before implementation. Read-only. Part of the /plan-review fan-out. ([Recon222/demo-website-dvr-extraction-notes](https://github.com/Recon222/demo-website-dvr-extraction-notes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/plan-reality-checker.md))
- **plan-reasoning-writer**: Use this agent when the user needs to document the rationale behind decisions made in `.claude/plan.md`, specifically explaining why the selected methods/approaches are more suitable than alternatives, and write this reasoning to `.claude/reasoning.md`. &lt;example&gt;Context: The user has finalized a plan and wants to document why certain approaches were chosen over alternatives. user: ".claude/plan.md에서 선택된 방법들이 다른 방법에 비해 적합한 근거를 /.claude/reasoning.md에 작성해줘" assistant: "I'm going to use the Agent tool to launch the plan-reasoning-writer agent to analyze the plan and document the reasoning for eac… ([SkyInMoon/utils-plan](https://github.com/SkyInMoon/utils-plan/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/plan-reasoning-writer.md))
- **plan-reconciler**: Reconciles a plan document against the conversation that produced it. Reports what the plan captured, what it missed, and what was glossed over. Read-only — never edits the plan. ([ohnotnow/agentic-stuff](https://github.com/ohnotnow/agentic-stuff/blob/HEAD/agents/plan-reconciler.md) · ★1)
- **plan-recuperar-confianza**: Ordena lo ocurrido en un conflicto con un cliente, tu equipo o un socio, detecta qué tipo de confianza se rompió y prepara un plan a 7, 30 y 90 días con un borrador del mensaje de reconocimiento. Úsalo cuando haya desconfianza, una queja seria o un error tuyo que arreglar. Solo redacta: no envía nada y no sustituye a un mediador ni a un abogado. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/negocio-clientes/agents/plan-recuperar-confianza.md))
- **plan-recuperar-confianza-2**: Ordena qué ha pasado con un cliente o con el equipo y prepara un plan para reconocer el fallo, repararlo y demostrar con hechos que algo cambia. Úsalo ante un conflicto o desconfianza. Redacta borradores y no envía nada. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/negocio-alternativas/agents/plan-recuperar-confianza-2.md))
- **plan-red-team**: Adversarial pre-mortem on an implementation plan before it executes — argues the strongest case that the plan is wrong. Dispatched once per plan at the plan gate by writing-plans. Not for reviewing code that already exists. ([mpeciakk/nixfiles](https://github.com/mpeciakk/nixfiles/blob/HEAD/config/claude/agents/plan-red-team.md))
- **plan-red-team**: Subagente adversário especializado em encontrar falhas lógicas, furos de arquitetura, regressões e riscos reais em planos de implementação. ([rdias612/llm_customizations](https://github.com/rdias612/llm_customizations/blob/HEAD/antigravity/squad/agents/plan-red-team.md))
- **plan-red-team**: Critique o plano com base no código e na escala reais. Leia o plano e confirme assinaturas, tipos, imports, rotas e consumidores citados. Não modifique o plano nem código; escreva somente no relatório Red atribuído. Não delegue. ([rdias612/llm_customizations](https://github.com/rdias612/llm_customizations/blob/HEAD/codex/agent-orchestrator/references/agents/plan-red-team.md))
- **plan-red-team-gate**: Reusable reviewer prompt for blockers, risky ambiguity, missing validation, hidden assumptions, and scope creep. ([Hugloss/agentsCookbook](https://github.com/Hugloss/agentsCookbook/blob/HEAD/agents/plan-red-team-gate.md))
- **plan-red-teamer**: Read-only critic for plan-workflow implementation plans. Applies skills/SHARED/PLAN_RED_TEAM_RUBRIC.md to an impl plan issue before phase-split and returns findings JSON per skills/SHARED/PLAN_RED_TEAM_FORMAT.md. Spawned by red-team-plan. ([threehillpath/marvin-sdd](https://github.com/threehillpath/marvin-sdd/blob/HEAD/agents/plan-red-teamer.md) · ★2)
- **plan-rederiver**: Read-only independent planner. Derives a technical plan from requirements alone so you can diff it against your own and find blind spots, missed areas, and unstated risks. Must never be shown the existing plan. Use on any draft plan, and note that the /feature pipeline dispatches it automatically at Step 2. ([dakotahp/dotfiles](https://github.com/dakotahp/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/dot_config/claude/agents/plan-rederiver.md))
- **plan-redteam-lead**: Skeptical engineering team lead for the plan-redteam skill. Attacks a written design spec or implementation plan from every angle with concrete evidence, then debates the author's per-attack defense and returns a final verdict per attack plus an agreed change list. Dispatch with a frozen brief (document paths, evidence paths, fixed user requirements, conventions); never pass conversation history. Continue the same agent with the author's defense. ([RoyAbra27/cc-jig](https://github.com/RoyAbra27/cc-jig/blob/HEAD/agents/plan-redteam-lead.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **plan-reducer**: Razona sobre un plan de tareas completo — lo dibuja desde cero o resuelve los veredictos de revisores paralelos en un plan único y coherente — y lo devuelve como JSON. Solo lectura, nunca escribe tasks.md. Pensado para el workflow tasks-fanout. ([rarango10/GoHarness-es](https://github.com/rarango10/GoHarness-es/blob/HEAD/plugin/goharness/agents/plan-reducer.md) · ★2)
- **plan-reducer**: Reasons about a complete task plan — draws it from scratch or resolves the verdicts of parallel reviewers into a single, coherent plan — and returns it as JSON. Read only, never writes tasks.md. Meant for the tasks-fanout workflow. ([rarango10/GoHarness](https://github.com/rarango10/GoHarness/blob/HEAD/plugin/goharness/agents/plan-reducer.md))
- **plan-refactor**: Analyzes code and returns prioritized refactoring recommendations without making changes. Use when the user asks what to refactor, where to improve code quality, or for analysis of refactoring opportunities. ([xdg/xdg-claude](https://github.com/xdg/xdg-claude/blob/HEAD/refactoring-agent/agents/plan-refactor.md) · ★20)
- **plan-refactor-rewrite-advisor**: Compares incremental refactor vs clean rewrite approaches for code changes ([eai-support/eai](https://github.com/eai-support/eai/blob/HEAD/.gemini/agents/plan-refactor-rewrite-advisor.md) · ★1 · 17 copies)
- **plan-referee**: Leia o plano e os relatórios Red e Blue. Julgue cada item pela evidência, conferindo no código divergências factuais. Não altere plano ou código; escreva somente no relatório de arbitragem atribuído. Não delegue. ([rdias612/llm_customizations](https://github.com/rdias612/llm_customizations/blob/HEAD/codex/agent-orchestrator/references/agents/plan-referee.md))
- **plan-referee**: Subagente árbitro e juiz técnico imparcial que analisa o debate entre red e blue team e consolida o veredito final em duas seções estruturadas. ([rdias612/llm_customizations](https://github.com/rdias612/llm_customizations/blob/HEAD/antigravity/squad/agents/plan-referee.md))
- **plan-reference-contract**: Internal planning sub-agent for reference_contract generation and readiness scoring. ([haolange/RDC-AgentCaps](https://github.com/haolange/RDC-AgentCaps/blob/HEAD/debugger/platforms/claude-code/.claude/agents/plan_reference_contract.md) · ★15)
- **plan-reference-contract**: Planning sub-agent for reference_contract generation and readiness scoring. Use this agent during the Plan/Intake Phase for contract generation and readiness assessment. ([haolange/RDC-AgentCaps](https://github.com/haolange/RDC-AgentCaps/blob/HEAD/debugger/platforms/copilot-ide/agents/plan_reference_contract.agent.md) · ★15)
- **plan-refine-scout**: Plan-refine scout agent — reads a backlog issue, assesses what artifacts exist, and executes the next workflow stage (research/plan/refine). Dispatched by /workflow-scope. - **plan-refiner**: Normalise a raw plan (markdown or freeform text from ExitPlanMode or a user) into the canonical cc-memory JSON schema. - **plan-refiner**: Review a plan document for gaps, improvements, and refinements ([aRustyDev/agents](https://github.com/aRustyDev/agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/plans/plugins/planning-workflow/scaffold/agents/plan-refiner.md) · ★8)
- **plan-refiner**: Refines existing high-level implementation plans based on user feedback, generating variants and hybrids while maintaining structure and updating trade-off analysis ([otrebu/agents](https://github.com/otrebu/agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/development-lifecycle/archive/agents/plan-refiner.md) · ★4)
- **plan-refiner**: Evaluates plans for elegant, maintainable implementations. Suggests alternatives only with high confidence. ([stephendolan/dotfiles](https://github.com/stephendolan/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/ai/agents/plan-refiner.md) · ★2)
- **plan-refiner**: Planning and instruction-refinement specialist. - **plan-refiner**: Orchestrates the plan review-edit loop.
